Result: Oracle实验显示高R提示词带来更大OPD增益，降序R训练优于随机和升序；代理分数分箱后平均R单调上升，验证其有效性；ReOrder-OPD在Qwen3/Gemma4数学任务及Qwen3代码任务的所有匹配对比中均提升性能，且在FiRe-OPD和ExOPD六个设置中均有增益，证明提示词排序与轨迹内监督互补。

Conclusion: 提示词级可靠性排序是一种即插即用的改进，能显著提升OPD效果，且与现有轨迹级过滤方法正交互补，为在线策略蒸馏提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) applies token-level teacher supervision to student-generated trajectories, but this supervision is not always reliable. Existing methods use local confidence or teacher-student agreement to weight, filter, or truncate the sampled trajectory. These signals do not directly determine whether the teacher can continue a student prefix to a correct answer, and trajectory-level interventions can conflate one rollout's unreliability with low expected training value of its prompt. We define prompt-level teacher continuation reliability $R$ as the teacher's probability of reaching a correct answer from a student prefix, averaged over prefixes and trajectories induced by the current student. Oracle experiments show that high-$R$ prompts yield larger OPD gains and that descending-$R$ training outperforms random and ascending orders on a fixed prompt pool. Because estimating $R$ requires many teacher continuations, we use the maximum ROUGE-5 F1 between one independent student rollout and verifier-correct same-prompt teacher trajectories. Across ten equal-frequency bins of this actual score, mean $R$ rises monotonically, showing that the proxy separates coarse reliability levels. ReOrder-OPD sorts prompts by the proxy, then draws independent on-policy training trajectories for vanilla OPD. It improves every matched aggregate comparison across Qwen3 and Gemma4 mathematics settings and Qwen3 code settings. Gains in all six FiRe-OPD and ExOPD settings show that prompt ordering complements within-trajectory supervision.

</details>


### [209] [Physics-informed Diffusion Generative Model for Time-Series Data Synthesis in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10941)
*Haiteng Wang,Yunfei Zhu,Tao Wang,Yikang Li,Jiabao Dong,Xiaoge Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysDGM，一种逐步嵌入物理定律的扩散生成模型，用于合成符合动力学系统物理规律的工业时间序列数据。该模型在反向扩散的每一步嵌入物理约束，保证轨迹级物理一致性。构建的440万样本合成数据集使下游任务性能大幅提升（剩余寿命预测+48%、健康指标估计+15%、健康状态评估+22%、故障诊断+20%），且所需训练数据减少10-20倍，验证了在航空发动机早期故障识别中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时序信号（如航空发动机涡轮温度、转速）对监测复杂动力系统健康状态至关重要，但受限于恶劣环境（高温高压）和高昂实验成本，数据采集困难。现有生成方法难以保证生成轨迹的物理一致性，导致下游任务泛化能力不足。

Method: PhysDGM将物理定律直接嵌入扩散模型的每一步反向生成过程中，而非仅在最终输出施加约束。通过逐步物理嵌入机制，确保生成轨迹在整个时间演化过程中满足动力学系统的物理约束，实现轨迹级物理一致性。

Result: 在涵盖涡扇发动机、航空发动机、电池、化工过程的34个数据集上，PhysDGM生成的440万样本合成数据使下游任务性能显著超越仅用真实数据：剩余寿命预测提升48%、健康指标估计提升15%、健康状态评估提升22%、故障诊断提升20%。所需训练数据量减少10-20倍，并成功应用于航空发动机早期故障识别。

Conclusion: PhysDGM为生成物理一致的工业时序数据奠定了坚实基础，展示了物理引导AI在数据稀缺环境（工业机械、复杂化学反应动力学等）扩展应用的广阔前景。

Abstract: Industrial time-series signals, such as turbine temperature and rotational speed in aero-engines, are essential for monitoring the health and operational status of complex dynamical systems. However, collecting such data is often limited by harsh environments (e.g., high temperature and high pressure) and the high cost of experimental testing. To address this challenge, we introduce PhysDGM, a stepwise physics-embedded diffusion generative model for synthesizing time-series data that are consistent with the underlying physical laws of dynamical systems. PhysDGM embeds physical laws directly into each reverse diffusion step of the generative process, ensuring trajectory-level physical consistency, rather than enforcing constraints only at the final output. A large-scale AI-synthetic dataset (4.4 million samples, 20x scale-up) constructed by PhysDGM demonstrates strong fidelity across 34 datasets spanning turbofan engines, aero-engines, batteries, and chemical processes. After incorporating the synthetic data, the downstream task performance substantially surpassed that using real data alone by 48% for remaining useful life prediction, 15% for health indicator estimation, 22% for state-of-health assessment, and 20% for fault diagnosis. Moreover, it requires 10-20x less training data than existing approaches, substantially reducing the high cost of data collection in dynamical systems. We further demonstrate PhysDGM's potential in identifying early-stage faults in aero-engines by incorporating AI-synthesized data. In summary, PhysDGM provides a solid foundation for generating physically consistent industrial time-series, paving the way for expanding physics-guided AI into diverse data-scarce environments, including both industrial machinery and complex chemical reaction dynamics.

</details>


### [210] [GARLIC: Graph Attention-based Relational Learning of Multivariate Time Series in Intensive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10969)
*Ruirui Wang,Yanke Li,Manuel Günther,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GARLIC模型针对ICU异构多变量时间序列的缺失和不规则采样，提出基于图注意力的关系学习框架，实现高精度且可解释的预测，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU数据具有异构、多变量、不规则采样和普遍缺失的特点，临床应用需要既准确又可解释的预测模型，现有方法难以同时满足。

Method: GARLIC包含可学习指数衰减编码器插补缺失数据，时滞摘要图捕捉传感器间依赖，跨维度序列注意力融合全局模式，所有注意力权重和图边端到端学习作为内置解释；采用交替解耦优化协调重建与分类目标。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、2019和MIMIC-III三个ICU基准上达到新SOTA，显著提升AUROC和AUPRC；消融实验验证各模块贡献；特征移除试验验证重要性归因保真度；实时病例研究展示可操作的风险预警及透明解释；在ICU外时间序列数据集上也表现优异。

Conclusion: GARLIC为不规则采样ICU时间序列数据提供了准确、可解释的深度学习解决方案，具有泛化性，推动了临床可解释AI的发展。

Abstract: Healthcare data, such as Intensive Care Unit (ICU) records, comprise heterogeneous multivariate time series sampled at irregular intervals with pervasive missingness. However, clinical applications demand predictive models that are both accurate and interpretable. We present our Graph Attention-based Relational Learning for Intensive Care (GARLIC) model, a novel neural network architecture that imputes missing data through a learnable exponential-decay encoder, captures inter-sensor dependencies via time-lagged summary graphs, and fuses global patterns with cross-dimensional sequential attention. All attention weights and graph edges are learned end-to-end to serve as built-in observation-, signal-, and edge-level explanations. To reconcile auxiliary reconstruction and primary classification objectives, we developed an alternating decoupled optimization scheme that stabilizes training. On three ICU benchmarks (PhysioNet 2012 & 2019, MIMIC-III), GARLIC sets the new state of the art in outcome prediction, significantly improving AUROC and AUPRC over best-performing baselines at comparable computational cost. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of each module, and feature-removal trials validate the fidelity of importance attribution through a monotonic performance drop (full > top 50% > random 50% > bottom 50%). Real-time case studies demonstrate actionable risk warnings with transparent explanations, marking a significant advance toward accurate, explainable deep learning for irregularly sampled ICU time series data. Moreover, we demonstrated \proposed{}'s superiority in data imputation and classification on various time-series datasets beyond the ICU domain, showing its generalizability and applicability to broader tasks.

</details>


### [211] [DEFT: Data-Efficient Frequency-domain Top-k Sampling via Inverse Discrete Fourier Transform for Spatiotemporal Dynamical Systems Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11019)
*Hengbo Xiao,Jiale Liu,Jiahao Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEFT是一种频域数据采样方法，通过识别主导傅里叶模式并系统变化振幅相位生成物理一致训练数据，显著降低数据需求并提高泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决PDE建模中物理模拟器计算昂贵和纯数据驱动模型泛化差的问题，提出一种高效生成物理一致训练数据的方法。

Method: 基于频域的数据采样方法DEFT，识别系统主导傅里叶模式，系统变化对应振幅和相位，通过逆离散傅里叶变换生成物理一致训练数据；推广界限推导提供选择K的理论准则。

Result: 在经典PDE上优于传统方法；在PDEBench扩散吸附和Burgers方程上数据需求减少40%且精度损失<2%；在电池降解PDE系统上R^2>0.99，频域特征迁移到其他电池化学体系仅需20%微调数据。

Conclusion: DEFT是一种有效的算子学习数据采样方法，能高效生成物理一致训练数据，显著降低数据需求并保持高预测精度，且具有良好的跨化学体系迁移能力。

Abstract: Modeling spatiotemporal dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) poses two major challenges: it either requires expensive physics-based simulators that entail iterative numerical solving at high computational cost, or it depends on abundant training data, yet purely data-driven models often generalize poorly to downstream dynamic operating conditions. We propose DEFT, a frequency-domain data sampling method that identifies the dominant Fourier modes of a physical system and systematically varies the corresponding amplitudes and phases to generate physically consistent training data via the inverse discrete Fourier transform. In addition, we derive a generalization bound of this method. We note that it also provides a theoretically principled criterion for selecting $K$. We evaluate the proposed method through three sets of experiments, each targeting a distinct aspect of its utility. First, we validate the framework on canonical PDEs solving demonstrating that it outperforms traditional methods when the system is dominated by a few prominent frequency components. Second, we employ DEFT as a data-value filter on the diffusion--sorption and Burgers equations of PDEBench, showing that it reduces data requirements by $40\%$ while sacrificing less than $2\%$ in predictive accuracy. Third, to evaluate DEFT for more challenging and practically relevant problems, we validate it in the battery degradation PDE system, achieving consistently high predictive accuracy across various test datasets with $R^2$ values exceeding $0.99$. Moreover, the learned frequency-domain features transfer to other battery chemistries with only $20\%$ of the fine-tuning data. These results demonstrate that DEFT is an effective data-sampling method for efficient operator learning.

</details>


### [212] [Derivative Computation in PINNs: Automatic Differentiation, Finite Differences and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11020)
*Maciej J. Mikulski,Tadeusz Uhl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究有限差分(FD)在物理信息神经网络(PINNs)中作为自动微分(AD)替代方案的可行性，证明经校准步长后FD精度匹配AD、速度更快、显存占用更低，并提出随机变体在定常问题上超越AD；同时指出对于含样本间依赖结构(如BatchNorm、自注意力)的网络，标准PyTorch autograd存在静默错误，FD提供前向仅近似，比真实逐样本导数近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在PINNs中用有限差分替代自动微分，以解决AD在大批量、含样本间依赖架构下的计算开销大、显存占用高、且逐样本梯度计算不可行等问题。

Method: 在三个基准PDE上对比FD与AD，校准步长；提出随机有限差分变体；分析含BatchNorm、自注意力等样本间依赖架构下标准autograd的错误，并验证FD前向近似的有效性。

Result: 经校准步长后，FD在所有测试问题上精度匹配AD，全批量范围内运行更快且显存占用显著降低；随机FD变体在定常问题上优于AD；对于样本间依赖架构，标准autograd静默错误，FD前向近似比真实逐样本导数近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 有限差分是PINNs中自动微分的有竞争力替代方案，尤其适合大批量训练和含样本间依赖的神经架构，能同时实现高精度、高速度、低显存，并修正标准autograd在逐样本导数计算上的缺陷。

Abstract: We systematically investigate finite-difference (FD) derivative computation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) as an alternative to automatic differentiation (AD). On three benchmark PDEs we show that, with a properly calibrated step size, FD matches AD in accuracy on every problem while running faster across the full tested batch-size range and using substantially less GPU memory, and that a stochastic variant we propose outperforms AD on a stationary problem. We further show that for neural architectures with inter-sample dependencies (e.g. BatchNorm, self-attention) the standard PyTorch autograd idiom is silently incorrect; the correct per-sample alternative is computationally infeasible at PINN-relevant batch sizes, while FD provides a forward-only approximation that is empirically an order of magnitude closer to the true per-sample derivative.

</details>


### [213] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Fisher 信息矩阵的双层优化方法来解决逆强化学习（IRL）中的计算难题，通过流式谱草图近似逆 Fisher-向量积，避免了显式构建大规模 Fisher 矩阵，在离散和连续控制环境中实现了具有竞争力的策略性能和奖励排序质量，同时降低了曲率存储复杂度并提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 IRL 表述为双层优化问题时，外层更新需要计算涉及内层目标逆 Hessian-向量积的超梯度，这在实践中计算极具挑战性，特别是对于大规模问题。

Method: 1. 证明在内层最优解处，内层目标的 Hessian 矩阵与策略的 Fisher 信息矩阵成正比，从而得到结构化的基于 Fisher 的超梯度，这与自然超梯度下降密切相关。2. 为解决大规模 Fisher 矩阵带来的可扩展性瓶颈，采用流式谱草图近似所需的逆 Fisher-向量积，避免显式构建 Fisher 矩阵。

Result: 在离散和连续控制环境中，与一阶随机双层基线相比，该方法展现出具有竞争力的策略性能和强奖励排序质量；Fisher 草图技术降低了曲率存储复杂度，且相对于显式 Fisher 求解器可提高计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过利用 Fisher 信息矩阵的结构特性和流式谱草图近似技术，本文有效解决了双层 IRL 中的超梯度计算难题，实现了可扩展、高效的奖励函数学习，为大规模 IRL 问题提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [214] [Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning for Genomics Applications: Insights from an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11054)
*Sepideh Saran,Mahsa Ghanbari,Uwe Ohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和蒙特卡洛Dropout三种不确定性量化方法在基因组学深度学习模型中的表现，发现贝叶斯神经网络在处理类别不平衡和分布外数据时表现更优，并展示了不确定性评分在蛋白质-RNA相互作用预测中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型已成为基因组学的标准计算工具，但不确定性量化（UQ）及其在该领域不同不确定性估计的可靠性受到的系统性关注很少，亟需建立系统评估框架来指导UQ方法在基因组学中的选择与应用。

Method: 通过实验对比深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和蒙特卡洛Dropout三种UQ方法，在序列到活性模型和单细胞表达分析两个基因组学应用领域及模态中，系统评估其在不同场景（包括类别不平衡、分布外数据等）下量化不确定性的能力。

Result: 贝叶斯神经网络尽管存在计算劣势，但在捕捉基因组学中由强类别不平衡和分布外数据引起的不确定性方面表现更优；不确定性评分可有效用于筛选蛋白质-RNA相互作用中的高质量预测。

Conclusion: 建立了基因组学深度学习模型不确定性量化方法的系统比较框架，为不同场景下UQ方法的适用性和可靠性提供了指导，揭示了各方法的优势与局限性，推动了基因组学中可靠不确定性估计的应用。

Abstract: Deep learning models have emerged as the standard computational tool for a wide range of applications in genomics. Yet, uncertainty quantification (UQ) -- and more specifically, the reliability of different uncertainty estimates in this domain -- has received little systematic attention. This work presents an empirical analysis of UQ in deep learning models, focusing on genomics applications. In a series of experiments, we contrast Deep Ensembles, Bayesian Neural Networks, and Monte Carlo-dropout methods. We assess their ability to quantify uncertainty in different scenarios, accounting for common dataset characteristics in two genomic application areas and modalities: sequence-to-activity models, and single-cell expression analysis. Our systematic comparison framework provides guidelines for the applicability and reliability of UQ methods in genomics, highlighting their strengths and limitations in different scenarios. We show that Bayesian Neural Networks are better at capturing uncertainty caused by strong class imbalance and out-of-distribution data in genomics, despite their computational disadvantages. Moreover, we show how uncertainty scores can be used to select high-quality predictions in protein-RNA interactions.

</details>


### [215] [Batch Size or Negatives? A Selection Rule for Memory-Constrained Recommender Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11061)
*Artyom Sabitov,Daniil Volkov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在固定内存预算下，采样softmax训练中批量大小与负样本数的权衡，理论证明并实验验证：应优先增大批量、减少负样本（n~B, k~1），以加快收敛并提升推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统全词表softmax内存开销大（O(nK)），采样softmax虽降至O(nk)，但在固定预算B=nk下，如何分配n和k以最优收敛尚不明确，需理论指导。

Method: 在标准平滑性和方差假设下，理论分析采样softmax的收敛速度，推导最优分配策略；在合成数据及四个真实序列推荐基准（含MovieLens-20M）上进行控制实验验证。

Result: 理论表明最快收敛来自n~B, k~1的分配；实验显示该配置在相同内存约束下收敛更快、最终推荐质量优于不平衡替代方案。

Conclusion: 为大规模推荐系统训练内存配置提供了理论与实证基础，建议在计算约束下尽可能包含更多样本（增大batch），而非增加负样本数。代码与复现材料已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale neural recommender systems are typically trained with a softmax cross-entropy objective over the full item vocabulary. For a typical large number of possible items $K$, the final classification layer dominates memory, requiring $O(nK)$ logits and gradients to materialize for a batch of $n$ examples. Sampled softmax reduces this cost by restricting the objective to only $k \ll K$ candidate negative items, resulting in an $O(nk)$ memory. However, for a fixed budget $B = n k$, it remains unclear whether one should prioritize larger batches or the inclusion of more negative items.
  We address this question by analyzing sampled-softmax training under a fixed memory constraint. Under standard smoothness and variance assumptions, our theoretical evidence suggests that the fastest convergence arises from an $ n \sim B, k \sim 1$ allocation. So, an actionable rule is to include as many objects as possible given computational constraints.
  Our theory is supported by controlled synthetic and synthetic and four real sequential recommendation benchmarks, including MovieLens-20M. The suggested configuration achieve faster convergence and better final recommendation quality than imbalanced alternatives within the same memory constraint. These findings provide a theoretical and empirical foundation for configuring memory during the training of recommender systems. Code, reproducibility materials, and all scripts for generating figures are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LimitedMemoryRule-BBFB

</details>


### [216] [Cross-View Feature Matching: Survey, Benchmarking, and Foundation-Model Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11093)
*Songlin Du,Xiaoyong Lu,Zeyu Wu,Xiaobo Lu,Guobao Xiao,Bin Fan,Jiayi Ma,Takeshi Ikenaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇关于跨视角特征匹配的综述，构建了统一的分类体系，涵盖特征提取、单/多类型匹配器、基于视觉基础模型的方法、训练策略与鲁棒估计，并提供了统一实验基准与开放挑战分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角特征匹配研究在问题定义、模型架构、训练范式和评估协议上高度多样化，缺乏统一的理解框架，难以进行公平对比和系统分析，亟需一份结构化的综述来梳理领域演进与现状。

Method: 提出结构化分类体系，涵盖特征提取、单类型特征匹配器、多类型特征匹配器、基于视觉基础模型的方法、训练策略与鲁棒估计六大维度；梳理近年进展，提炼关键设计原则，指出向统一泛化匹配模型转变的趋势；建立统一实验基准，在一致协议下对比代表性最先进方法性能；讨论效率、极端条件鲁棒性、跨域泛化等开放挑战与未来方向。

Result: 提供了跨视角特征匹配领域的全面结构化参考，实现了对代表性方法的公平全面性能对比，明确了关键设计原则与发展趋势，并指出了效率、极端鲁棒性、跨域泛化等核心挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在为视觉基础模型时代的跨视角特征匹配提供全面且结构化的参考，帮助理解领域演进、当前格局及未来发展。

Abstract: Cross-view feature matching aims to establish reliable correspondences across images with large viewpoint variations. Over the past decade, the field has evolved from task-specific models toward increasingly unified and generalizable correspondence models, with recent progress further driven by the emergence of vision foundation models (VFMs). Despite these advances, existing studies remain highly diverse in their problem formulations, model architectures, training paradigms, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to obtain a unified understanding of the field. In this survey, we present a unified review of cross-view feature matching. We first introduce a structured taxonomy covering feature extraction, single-type feature matcher, multi-type feature matcher, VFMs based methods, training strategy and robust estimation, providing a coherent framework for analysis and comparison. We further examine recent advances, distilling key design principles and highlighting the shift toward unified and generalizable correspondence models. We also provide a unified experimental benchmarking of representative state-of-the-art methods under consistent protocols, enabling fair and comprehensive performance comparisons. In addition, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including efficiency, robustness under extreme conditions, and cross-domain generalization. This survey aims to provide a comprehensive and structured reference for understanding the evolution, current landscape, and future development of cross-view feature matching in the era of vision foundation models.

</details>


### [217] [Two-stage Odd Residual Flows for Mean-Preserving Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11114)
*Kiran Madhusudhanan,Christian Klötergens,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双阶段奇数残差流(TORF)，通过解耦均值预测与不确定性估计，实现高精度确定性预测与灵活概率建模的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测方法在分布灵活性与均值预测准确性之间存在根本权衡：传统参数化方法(如MVE)在联合NLL目标下点预测精度退化；现代生成模型(归一化流、扩散模型)依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样且均值估计往往次优。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用预训练确定性模型产生准确均值预测；第二阶段采用受限归一化流(严格奇函数)学习围绕点预测的灵活残差分布，理论保证均值保持且无需采样。

Result: 实验表明TORF在确定性精度(NMAE)达到最优水平，同时在短期和长期预测任务中提供强密度估计性能(CRPS)。

Conclusion: TORF有效解决了分布灵活性与均值预测准确性的权衡，为风险敏感决策提供了无需采样的高效概率预测框架。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting plays an essential role in risk-sensitive decision-making, particularly in long-horizon settings. However, existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between distributional flexibility and accurate mean prediction. Traditional parametric methods, such as Mean Variance Estimation (MVE), can suffer from degraded point accuracy when trained under joint Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) objectives, while modern-flexible generative models, including Normalizing Flows and Diffusion Models, typically rely on costly Monte Carlo sampling and may yield suboptimal mean estimates. To address this limitation, we propose Two-stage Odd Residual Flows (TORF), a framework that decouples mean forecasting from uncertainty estimation. In the first stage, a pre-trained deterministic model is used to produce an accurate mean prediction. In the second stage, a Restricted Normalizing Flow, with strictly odd functions learns flexible residual distributions around the point forecast, guaranteeing mean preservation from the first stage without sampling. Experiments show that TORF achieves state-of-the-art deterministic accuracy (NMAE) while providing strong density estimation performance (CRPS) on short and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [218] [A Recommendation System Approach for Interference-Robust Sensor Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11143)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Kyle Pak,Merve Karakas,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于推荐系统的传感器子集选择方法，利用频带声学特征和双塔多层感知机（MLP）架构，在户外车辆跟踪部署中将跟踪精度比RSSI基线提升约20%，同时保持低计算开销以满足实时选择性感知需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RSSI的传感器子集选择方法虽然高效，但易受声学干扰影响，限制了跟踪精度。需要一种更鲁棒的声学特征表示和高效评分机制来提升选择性感知的跟踪性能。

Method: 提出基于推荐系统的框架，使用频带声学特征替代RSSI，采用双塔MLP架构对候选传感器子集进行高效评分。双塔结构分别处理传感器特征和目标/环境特征，通过内积或相似度计算快速评估子集质量。

Result: 在户外车辆跟踪实验部署中，该方法跟踪准确率比RSSI基线提升约20%，同时保持低计算开销，满足实时选择性感知要求。

Conclusion: 基于频带声学特征和双塔MLP的推荐系统框架有效解决了RSSI方法对声学干扰敏感的问题，显著提升了传感器子集选择的跟踪精度，且计算效率高，适用于实时选择性感知场景。

Abstract: This paper develops a method for sensor-subset selection for tracking. Prior work showed that low-cost acoustic Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) measurements can be used to recommend subsets of sensor nodes whose expensive sensing modalities, such as cameras, can achieve high tracking accuracy. While efficient, RSSI-based approaches are challenged by acoustic interference. We propose a recommendation-system-inspired framework that instead leverages frequency-band acoustic features and a Two-Tower Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) architecture to efficiently score candidate sensor subsets. Experimental results on outdoor vehicle-tracking deployments show that the proposed method can improve accuracy by around 20\% over the RSSI baseline while maintaining the low computational overhead required for real-time selective sensing.

</details>


### [219] [DACRI: Decision-Aware Causal Intervention Ranking for Critical Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11154)
*Shiqi Huang,Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Jize Li,Yan Lyu,Lashimi Muraleedharan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CriticalSCM-Bench v1基准，评估供应链中断干预策略，发现LambdaMART在半导体和关键材料领域优于静态基准，但在数字基础设施领域简单缓冲策略更优，且模型复杂度不总是合理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有供应链中断检测/归因方法不等同于选择最大化可恢复净值的干预措施，需要一个具有因果真值、配对事实/反事实推演和显式净值目标的基准来评估干预策略。

Method: 构建CriticalSCM-Bench v1合成基准，包含因果真值、配对事实/反事实推演、显式净值目标；使用LambdaMART学习排序模型与全信息静态基准、领域知情常数缓冲策略对比；在部分信息、延迟设置、压力测试（干预保真度、时机、成本、分布外中断）下评估；另进行守护解释研究，540代生成后确定性验证和模板回退。

Result: LambdaMART较静态基准提升中位数归一化净值5.7-16.2%，在半导体和关键材料原型上成对统计显著，数字基础设施上不显著；常数缓冲策略在数字基础设施更强；部分/延迟设置下LambdaMART保留33-75%全钳制值；压力测试显示干预保真度、时机、成本、分布外中断改变策略排序；关键材料分布外保留最弱；守护解释研究保留所有固定干预决策，但措辞不稳定。

Conclusion: 自适应排序在特定情境下增加价值，但在其他情境下简单结构化策略更优；模型复杂度并非普遍合理；基准揭示了不同供应链原型的干预策略适用范围。

Abstract: Detecting or attributing a supply-chain disruption is not the same as selecting the intervention that maximizes recoverable net value. We present CriticalSCM-Bench v1, a controlled synthetic benchmark with causal ground truth, paired factual/counterfactual rollouts, and an explicit net-value objective. Relative to a full-information train-selected static benchmark, LambdaMART improves median normalized net value by 5.7--16.2\%, with paired statistical support on the semiconductor and critical-material archetypes but not on digital infrastructure. On digital infrastructure, a domain-informed constant-buffer policy remains stronger, showing that greater model complexity is not uniformly justified. Across partial and delayed settings, LambdaMART retains 33--75\% of full-clamp value. Stress tests further show that intervention fidelity, timing, cost, and held-out disruptions can alter policy ordering. Critical materials show the weakest out-of-distribution retention. Separately, a guarded explanation study over 540 generations preserves every fixed intervention decision after deterministic validation and template fallback, although exact wording remains unstable. Within this controlled setting, the results identify regimes in which adaptive ranking adds value and those in which simpler structural policies remain preferable.

</details>


### [220] [Hierarchical Empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes: Minimax Smoothing and Calibration with AODE Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11162)
*Nguyen Thai Anh,Truong Viet Vu,Tran Thien Thanh,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Ngo Hoang Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出分层经验贝叶斯朴素贝叶斯（HEB-NB）及其扩展HEB-AODE，通过数据自适应学习狄利克雷先验浓度参数实现自适应平滑，理论建立非渐近L1误差界与超额贝叶斯风险界，实验在31个基准上显著提升概率预测准确性与校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有朴素贝叶斯平滑规则（如拉普拉斯、Lidstone、Krichevsky-Trofimov、m-estimate）均采用固定平滑强度，忽略特征基数、样本量与类别不平衡，导致在高基数表格数据上产生非消失偏差。

Method: 提出HEB-NB：为每个类别-特征条件概率引入狄利克雷先验，通过II型最大似然（经验贝叶斯）数据自适应学习浓度参数，实现跨类别原则性信息共享并保持闭式推断；进一步将自适应平滑迁移到结构松弛模型，构建HEB-AODE。

Result: 理论：建立非渐近L1误差界，匹配经验分布极小极大率加消失自适应偏差；给出匹配的拉普拉斯紧下界，证明有限样本风险层面严格优于拉普拉斯；推导插入式超额贝叶斯风险界与总体top-1期望校准误差（ECE）推论。实验：跨31个UCI/OpenML基准，HEB-NB在概率指标上获最优平均Friedman排名，高基数数据集log-loss降低达22.1%，HEB-AODE持续优于原版AODE；结合互信息加权使top-1 ECE降低41%-70%。

Conclusion: HEB-NB通过分层经验贝叶斯自适应平滑解决了固定平滑的偏差缺陷，在理论与实验上均实现概率准确性与校准性能的显著提升，且自适应机制可无缝迁移至AODE等结构松弛模型。

Abstract: The Naive Bayes (NB) classifier remains a standard choice for categorical data, yet its widely used smoothing rules, such as Laplace, Lidstone, Krichevsky-Trofimov, and the $m$-estimate, all prescribe a fixed smoothing strength that ignores feature cardinality, sample size, and class imbalance, inducing a non-vanishing bias on modern high-cardinality tabular data. We propose hierarchical empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes (HEB-NB), in which each class-feature conditional probability is smoothed by a Dirichlet prior whose concentration is learned data-adaptively via Type-II maximum likelihood, enabling principled information sharing across classes while retaining closed-form inference. We further introduce HEB average one-dependence estimators (HEB-AODE), showing that the adaptive smoothing transfers cleanly to structural relaxations of NB. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic $\ell_1$ error bound for HEB-NB matching the empirical-distribution minimax rate plus a vanishing data-adaptive bias, together with a matching Laplace-tight lower bound that yields a finite-sample, risk-level strict separation from Laplace. We further derive a plug-in excess Bayes-risk bound via total-variation tensorization and a population top-1 expected calibration error (ECE) corollary. Empirically, across 31 UCI and OpenML benchmarks, HEB-NB attains the best average Friedman rank on probabilistic metrics, with up to 22.1% log-loss reductions on high-cardinality datasets and consistent improvements of HEB-AODE over vanilla AODE. Combining HEB-NB with mutual-information weighting reduces top-1 ECE by 41%-70%, demonstrating substantial gains in probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering: Rethinking Graph Collaborative Filtering as Tree Collaborative Filtering with Curvature-Aware Propagation Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10297)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于树结构的神经树协同过滤（NTCF），利用离散Ricci曲率代理为每个节点分配特定传播深度，解决统一深度导致的过度平滑和欠探索问题，性能优于现有GCF模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCF使用统一传播深度，忽略节点局部连接度差异，导致边缘节点过度平滑、中心节点欠探索，限制了表示能力。

Method: 将每个节点的局部邻域视为有根树，基于闭式局部度不平衡分数（作为离散Ricci曲率代理）分配节点特定的传播深度，提出NTCF框架。

Result: 理论上证明NTCF严格推广NGCF，且曲率感知调度在正曲率节点保留更多判别信息；实验在三个公开数据集上显示NTCF优于主流GCF骨干，且可作为自监督模型骨干替换提升性能。

Conclusion: NTCF通过节点自适应传播深度有效解决过度平滑和欠探索，显著提升推荐性能，具有良好的理论保证和实用性。

Abstract: Graph Collaborative Filtering (GCF) has become the dominant paradigm in modern recommender systems by modeling user-item interactions as a bipartite graph and propagating embeddings through a fixed number of message-passing layers. However, applying a uniform propagation depth to every node ignores a fundamental property of real interaction graphs: nodes differ substantially in their local connectivity, so peripheral nodes quickly suffer from over-smoothing while hub-like nodes remain under-explored beyond their immediate neighborhood. In this paper, we revisit GCF from a tree-structured perspective and propose Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering (NTCF), a framework that re-interprets each node's local neighborhood as a rooted tree and assigns a node-specific propagation depth based on a closed-form local-degree-imbalance score that serves as a discrete Ricci-curvature proxy. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that (i) NTCF strictly generalizes NGCF, degenerating to NGCF when all curvature-induced depth adjustments vanish (a lower bound on its representation power), and (ii) the curvature-aware schedule retains strictly more discriminative information at deep layers on positively-curved (peripheral) nodes than uniform-depth propagation. NTCF can achieve higher performance than most widely used GCF backbone models and can be integrated into existing advanced self-supervised models as a backbone, replacing their original backbone to achieve enhanced performance. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of NTCF.

</details>


### [222] [Persona Conditioning as an Assessor-Sensitivity Probe for LLM-Based IR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10385)
*Samaneh Mohtadi,Pietro Bernardelle,Joel Mackenzie,Gianluca Demartini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过角色条件化探究大语言模型作为相关性评估者时的敏感性，发现评估敏感性具有结构性而非均匀性，主要表现为评估严格性、证据阈值或解释重点的偏移，而非大规模相关性反转。大模型保持系统排序一致性，小模型放大人格诱导的不稳定性。敏感性集中于特定检索系统及类型。角色条件化可作为受控敏感性探针，用于压力测试基于LLM的IR评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地被用作信息检索评估中的相关性评估者，需要了解评估者框架如何影响判断可靠性及下游系统比较，因此研究角色条件化作为诊断机制来暴露LLM评估者的敏感性。

Method: 从PersonaHub和NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA两个来源选取任务导向人格，实例化五种评估者角色（意图解释、领域专业知识、对比判断、证据验证、全局搜索质量评估），对比标准UMBRELA基线。在六个LLM主干模型上，使用TREC DL20和RAG24数据集进行分析。

Result: 分析揭示结构性而非均匀性的评估者敏感性。判断通常接近基线，但会改变评估严格性、证据阈值或解释重点，而非产生大规模相关性反转。系统层面，高容量模型保持系统排序一致性，小模型放大人格诱导的不稳定性。局部排名位移分析显示敏感性集中于特定检索系统和类型，特别是DL20上的神经排序/重排序系统和RAG24上的RAG导向流水线。人格来源影响小于评估者角色和模型容量。

Conclusion: 角色条件化判断可作为受控敏感性探针，用于压力测试基于LLM的IR评估流程，并识别对评估者框架敏感的系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as relevance assessors in information retrieval (IR) evaluation, raising questions about how assessor framing affects judgment reliability and downstream system comparison. We study persona conditioning as a diagnostic mechanism for exposing LLM assessor sensitivity. Using task-oriented personas drawn from two complementary sources (PersonaHub and NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA), we instantiate five assessor roles emphasizing intent interpretation, domain expertise, contrastive judgment, evidence verification, and global search-quality assessment, compared with a standard UMBRELA baseline. Across six LLM backbones on TREC DL20 and RAG24, our analyses reveal structured rather than uniform assessor sensitivity. Judgments usually remain close to the baseline while shifting assessment strictness, evidential threshold, or interpretation emphasis rather than producing widespread relevance reversals. At the system level, high-capacity models preserve system-ranking agreement, while smaller models amplify persona-induced instability. Local rank-displacement analysis shows sensitivity concentrates on particular retrieval systems and system types, especially neural ranking/reranking systems on DL20 and RAG-oriented pipelines on RAG24. Persona source matters less than assessor role and model capacity. These findings position persona-conditioned judging as a controlled sensitivity probe for stress-testing LLM-based IR evaluation pipelines and identifying systems whose evaluation outcomes are sensitive to assessor framing.

</details>


### [223] [Post-Calibration Reliability Reranking of Relevance Decisions via Label-wise Monotone Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10406)
*Inwoo Tae,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Label-wise Monotone Reliability Projection (MRP)方法，解决检索系统中校准后置信度仍存在标签依赖的可靠性差异问题。MRP学习标签级单调函数将校准置信度映射为正确性可靠性，保持原始预测标签和类概率，通过可靠性分数重排序降低残余风险。在六个信息获取相关性数据集上，MRP提升了可靠性重排序和平均回退效用，同时保持全覆盖准确率和ECE。消融实验表明主要增益来自标签级残余可靠性而非全局置信度重映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web搜索、产品搜索和问答检索系统为查询-候选对分配相关性标签和置信度分数。事后校准虽能使置信度与平均正确率对齐，但无法消除同一校准置信度水平下不同预测标签间的可靠性差异（即标签依赖的残余风险），导致系统可能过度信任错误预测或不必要地延迟正确预测。

Method: 提出Label-wise Monotone Reliability Projection (MRP)：为每个预测标签学习一个单调函数，将校准后的置信度映射为正确性可靠性分数，同时保持原始预测标签和类概率分布不变。该可靠性分数用于对固定预测进行重排序，以降低残余风险。方法兼容多种事后校准器，且可分析何时可将可靠性分数嵌回顶标签概率几何中。

Result: 在六个信息获取相关性数据集上，结合多种事后校准器，MRP显著提升了可靠性重排序性能（如NDCG、MAP）和平均回退效用，同时保持了全覆盖准确率和期望校准误差（ECE）不变。结构消融实验表明，主要性能增益来源于标签级残余可靠性建模，而非全局置信度重映射。

Conclusion: MRP有效解决了校准后置信度中残留的标签依赖可靠性差异，通过标签级单调映射生成可靠性分数实现风险感知重排序，在不牺牲原有准确性和校准质量的前提下提升系统实用性。该方法具有良好的兼容性和泛化性，代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Web search, product search, and question-answering retrieval systems often assign a relevance label and confidence score to each query-candidate pair. The relevance label describes how well a page, product, or passage matches the query, while the confidence often guides downstream use or fallback decisions. Post-hoc calibration is therefore needed because misaligned confidence can make systems over-trust wrong predictions or unnecessarily defer correct ones. However, calibration mainly aligns confidence with average correctness, and does not remove predicted-label-dependent reliability differences that remain within the same calibrated confidence level. We address this gap with Label-wise Monotone Reliability Projection (MRP), which learns label-wise monotone functions that map calibrated confidence to correctness reliability while preserving the original predicted labels and class probabilities. The resulting reliability score reranks fixed predictions according to residual risk. Across six information access relevance datasets and multiple post-hoc calibrators, MRP improves reliability reranking and average fallback utility while preserving full-coverage accuracy and ECE. Structural ablations show that the main gains come from label-wise residual reliability rather than from global confidence remapping. We further analyze when MRP reliability scores can be embedded back into top-label probability geometry, showing that this projection is useful as a compatibility analysis but is distinct from the main reliability-reranking objective. The implementation will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [224] [Towards Efficient Reasoning in LLM-Based Recommender Systems via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10447)
*Linh Dieu Le,Tong Chen,Shazia Sadiq,Hongzhi Yin,Ming Jin,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出首个基于模型融合的推荐系统推理压缩框架，通过注意力头级别的细粒度融合，将快思考模型的简洁行为注入慢思考模型，在保持推荐准确性的同时减少推理冗长度达24.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢思考模型虽准确但推理冗长，增加推理成本；现有训练压缩方法适应成本高，推理时方法脆弱难扩展；模型融合提供免训练的行为迁移方案，融合快慢思考模型可平衡准确性与简洁性。

Method: 提出细粒度模型融合框架，在注意力头级别分配不同融合系数，根据各头对关键推理证据的贡献和参数敏感性，选择性注入快思考模型的简洁行为到慢思考模型。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，推理长度减少高达24.3%，在保持推荐准确性方面优于竞争性模型融合基线。

Conclusion: 该工作首创基于注意力头细粒度融合的推理压缩方法，有效解决了推荐系统中慢思考模型推理冗长问题，提供免训练、可扩展的解决方案，代码开源。

Abstract: Large language model-based recommender systems are increasingly adopting slow-thinking models that generate step-by-step reasoning before making predictions, often achieving higher accuracy than fast-thinking models that predict directly. However, their reasoning traces are often unnecessarily verbose, increasing inference costs without commensurate accuracy gains. Existing training-based approaches to reasoning compression often incur substantial adaptation costs, while inference-time methods are brittle and difficult to scale. These limitations motivate model merging as a promising training-free direction for transferring specialised behaviours between models in a shared parameter space. In particular, merging a slow-thinking model with a fast-thinking counterpart provides a natural mechanism for balancing recommendation accuracy and reasoning conciseness. To this end, we propose, to our knowledge, the first model merging framework for reasoning compression in recommender systems. Unlike conventional merging methods that apply uniform merge coefficients across model components, our method performs fine-grained merging at the level of individual attention heads, capturing heterogeneous patterns in recommendation reasoning. Each attention head is assigned a distinct merge coefficient according to its contribution to critical reasoning evidence and its sensitivity to parameter change, enabling selective injection of the concise behaviour of the fast-thinking model into the slow-thinking model and reducing reasoning verbosity without compromising recommendation quality. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that our method reduces reasoning length by up to 24.3% while outperforming competitive model merging baselines in maintaining recommendation accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linhledieu/REAM.

</details>


### [225] [When Do Anchor-Based Pointwise LLM Rerankers Help? Retriever Quality, Statistical Scope, and Anchor Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10528)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文以复现为起点，对基于锚点的逐点式LLM重排序方法（GCCP/PAGC）进行了受控的组件级压力测试，发现核心对比评分思想稳健，但原论文固定的两个设计选择（融合标准相关性分数、复杂锚点构建）在更强稠密检索器下收益有限或无效，更简单的锚点构建方式表现相当或更优，且结论在不同LLM主干（含4-bit量化72B模型）下一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文报告的GCCP/PAGC方法在仅凭论文文本复现时nDCG@10仅为0.24，远低于报告的0.66，揭示了多个未记录的实现细节对结果影响巨大。作者旨在通过完整复现并验证实现后，系统性地评估该方法各组件的真实贡献及其适用边界。

Method: 1. 仅基于论文文本进行初始复现，量化复现差距。2. 识别并恢复八个未记录的实现细节，达到报告结果的1.6%以内。3. 使用验证后的实现，对两个固定设计选择进行受控消融：(a) 对比评分与标准逐点相关性分数的融合；(b) 复杂锚点构建与简单交错顶句锚点的对比。4. 跨多个数据集、不同一阶检索器（BM25、E5等稠密模型）及多种LLM主干（含4-bit量化72B）进行统计显著性测试。

Result: 核心对比评分机制在严格统计校正下表现稳健。融合标准相关性分数仅在BM25检索器下带来提升，在E5等强稠密检索器下收益微乎其微。复杂锚点构建方法无必要，简单交错顶句锚点在各数据集上匹配或超越原方法。上述发现跨不同LLM主干一致。

Conclusion: 基于锚点的逐点式重排序有效，但其增益主要源于对比评分，而非更复杂的聚合策略或锚点构建选择；且该方法的有效性条件比原评估所暗示的更窄（主要在弱一阶检索器下显著）。

Abstract: Anchor-based pointwise LLM reranking scores each candidate against a shared reference passage to recover cross-document context at pointwise cost. We study when this actually helps, using GCCP/PAGC as a representative method. Our study is reproduction-first. We use reproduction as a starting point for a controlled component-level stress test of anchor-based pointwise reranking. Our initial reimplementation, based only on the paper text, achieves 0.24 nDCG@10 instead of the reported 0.66, revealing that several undocumented implementation details are necessary to reproduce the method. After identifying and recovering eight such details, we reproduce the reported results within 1.6% and use the validated implementation for controlled analysis.
  We find that the core contrastive scoring idea is robust under rigorous statistical correction. However, two design choices held fixed in the original paper are less reliable. First, we find that combining the contrastive score with the standard pointwise relevance score helps when the first-stage retriever is BM25, but gives little or no benefit when the first-stage retriever is a stronger dense model such as E5. Second, the paper's more complex method for constructing the anchor is unnecessary. A much simpler anchor, built by interleaving the top-ranked sentences, matches or outperforms it across datasets. These findings are consistent across different LLM backbones, including a 4-bit quantized 72B model. Overall, anchor-based pointwise reranking is effective, but its gains come mainly from contrastive scoring rather than from the more complex aggregation and anchor-construction choices, and they appear under narrower conditions than the original evaluation suggests.

</details>


### [226] [Multi Interests for Joint Search-Recommendation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10535)
*Xiangchen Pan,Wei Wei,Huakang Niu,Zhicong Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIJSR框架，针对搜索推荐混合序列中多兴趣表达被忽视的问题，从结构和语义两视角挖掘多兴趣并自适应融合，在两个开源数据集上显著提升搜索推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域统一建模框架虽能弥补跨域行为差异，但忽略了混合序列下不同场景的兴趣表达差异，导致无法细粒度捕捉用户在搜索和推荐场景下的多样化兴趣。

Method: MIJSR包含三大模块：1)跨域行为融合：通过对比学习对齐查询与物品表示；2)多兴趣挖掘：结构视角利用子序列划分和掩码设置提取搜索兴趣、推荐兴趣及跨域兴趣；语义视角利用查询语义信息聚类并对混合序列进行语义分割构建语义多兴趣；3)多任务预测：自适应融合多兴趣与侧信息，采用渐进分层提取模型进行多任务预测。

Result: 在两个开源数据集上的大量实验表明，MIJSR通过细粒度多兴趣提取，在搜索和推荐任务上均显著优于基线模型，验证了结构与语义双视角多兴趣建模的有效性。

Conclusion: MIJSR框架成功解决了混合序列下多兴趣表达被忽视的问题，双视角多兴趣挖掘与自适应融合机制为联合搜索推荐建模提供了新范式，代码已开源供复现。

Abstract: Search and recommendation are crucial for understanding user preferences. More and more studies are attempting to jointly model search behavior and recommendation behavior, by integrating user active search and passive recommendation behavior data to better mine user preferences. However, although existing cross-domain unified modeling frameworks can effectively compensate for the differences in behavior between domains, they overlook the expression of interests in different scenarios under mixed sequences. In this study, we propose a multi-interest-based mixed sequential modeling framework MIJSR, which performs multi-interest mining and adaptive integration on search recommendation mixed sequences from both structural and semantic perspectives. Specifically, our model can be roughly divided into three modules: cross-domain behavior fusion, multi-interest mining, and multi-task prediction. Firstly, we align the representations of query and item through contrastive learning training. Then, we extract the multi interests of the mixed behavior sequence from both structural and semantic perspectives. Structurally, we extract search interests, recommendation interests, and cross interests through subsequence partitioning and mask settings; In terms of semantics, we use the semantic information of queries for clustering and perform semantic segmentation on mixed sequences to construct semantic multi interests. Finally, the adaptive fusion of multiple interests is combined with other side information to use a progressive layered extraction model for multi-task prediction. Extensive experiments on two open-source datasets have shown that our model can further enhance its accuracy in search and recommendation by extracting users' multi interests at a fine-grained level. Codes are available at https://github.com/pxcstart/MIJSR.

</details>


### [227] [DistilVDR: A Compact End-to-End Visual Document Retriever via Dual-Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10636)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Ziyi Wang,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DistilVDR是一个5.24亿参数的端到端视觉文档检索系统，通过双边蒸馏从8B教师模型获得，实现了单向量检索器，无需相关性标签、负采样或对比学习，两个变体在ViDoRe基准上表现优异，索引大小减少15.6倍，索引速度提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉文档检索（VDR）主要依赖数十亿参数的多向量模型，索引速度慢、服务成本高；现有压缩方法要么从头训练更小的多向量编码器，要么仅蒸馏查询侧，无法端到端得到紧凑的单向量检索器。

Method: 提出DistilVDR，采用不对称的仅编码器学生架构，从冻结的8B视觉语言教师模型通过点态余弦对齐损失进行双边蒸馏；所有监督来自教师的嵌入空间，无需相关性标签、负采样或对比项；将视觉容量集中在文档侧，查询侧仅保留70M参数；发布两个变体：DistilVDR-HiRes和DistilVDR-Fast，仅文档编码器的视觉瓦片预算不同。

Result: DistilVDR-HiRes在ViDoRe v1+v2+v3上达到平均NDCG@5 61.74（为8B教师的86.9%），在对高分辨率敏感的v3基准上超越所有复现的亚10亿参数基线；DistilVDR-Fast以3倍更小的视觉令牌预算达到59.98；两个变体均将百万文档索引大小减少15.6倍（相比最强亚10亿多向量基线），索引速度提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: DistilVDR展示了通过双边蒸馏和不对称架构，可以训练出紧凑高效的单向量视觉文档检索器，无需额外监督信号，显著降低索引存储和计算成本，同时保持竞争性检索性能，为大规模VDR部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval (VDR) is dominated by multi-billion-parameter models that are slow to index at full corpus scale and expensive to serve. Prior compression routes either train a smaller multi-vector encoder from scratch or distil only the query side; neither yields a compact single-vector retriever end-to-end. We present DistilVDR, a 524M end-to-end VDR system distilled bilaterally from a single 8B vision-language teacher under a pointwise cosine alignment loss. All supervision comes from the frozen teacher's embedding space, which was itself trained with relevance supervision, so the student objective needs no relevance labels, negative sampling, or contrastive term. We match VDR's text-query and image-document input asymmetry with an asymmetric encoder-only student that concentrates visual capacity on the document side and keeps the query side at 70M parameters. We release two variants that share the same encoders and training and differ only in the document encoder's visual-tile budget: DistilVDR-HiRes attains 61.74 average NDCG@5 on ViDoRe v1+v2+v3 (86.9% of the 8B teacher) and leads every reproduced sub-1B baseline on the high-resolution-sensitive v3 benchmark, while DistilVDR-Fast attains 59.98 at a 3 times smaller visual-token budget. Both variants store one million documents in a 15.6 times smaller index than the strongest sub-1B multi-vector baseline and index the corpus an order of magnitude faster. The code is available at https://github.com/Ryenhails/NanoVDR.

</details>


### [228] [ENTLORE: A Graph-Grounded Benchmark for Latent Organizational Reasoning in Enterprise Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10679)
*Akrin Zheng,Alexander Wu,Alaia Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ENTLORE基准，用于评估企业问答系统中潜在组织推理能力，发现即使提供金标准文档，隐性组织关系问题仍有30.4%无法回答，远高于显性查找(12.6%)和跨源组合(6.2%)，表明企业问答不仅依赖文档召回，更依赖隐性组织关系的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业问答基准主要测试已陈述事实的组合，而非从语料中缺失的目标关系恢复能力（即潜在组织推理）。企业常规记录中组织关系隐性分布于异构源，需要专门基准评估系统挖掘隐性关系的能力。

Method: 构建ENTLORE图基准框架：从常规文档、权威组织表、运营记录重建审计企业世界；用版本化组织惯例在真值图中认证派生关系，生成完整金标准答案和证明证书；发布对齐匿名化语料库，隐藏私有结构和目标关系。包含2341文档(三源)、907问题(显性查找/跨源组合/潜在推理)、56模型配置评测。

Result: 将发布世界结构化为诱导实体图或可导航知识库效果最强。但即使提供金标准文档，潜在推理问题仍有30.4%无法回答，显著高于显性查找(12.6%)和跨源组合(6.2%)。隐性组织关系的可用性是关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 企业问答不仅依赖文档召回，更取决于隐性组织关系是否可被利用。ENTLORE基准、数据、代码已公开，为评估潜在组织推理能力提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering is framed as retrieving internal documents and generating grounded answers. Routine enterprise records, however, are work by-products in which required organizational relations remain implicit across heterogeneous sources. Existing benchmarks provide realistic multi-source evidence, but often materialize a predefined answer path and therefore test the composition of stated facts rather than recovery of a target relation absent from the corpus. We call the latter capability latent organizational reasoning.
  We introduce ENTLORE, a graph-grounded benchmark construction framework that reconstructs an audited enterprise world from routine documents, authoritative organizational tables, and operational records. Versioned organizational conventions certify derived relations in a truth graph, enabling complete golden answers and proof certificates. The aligned anonymized release exposes only the document corpus while withholding private structure and target relations. ENTLORE contains 2,341 documents from three source types and 907 questions spanning explicit lookup, cross-source composition, and latent organizational reasoning, evaluated across 56 model and access configurations. Structuring the released world as an induced entity graph or navigable knowledge base gives the strongest deployable results. Yet supplying gold documents still leaves 30.4% of latent questions unanswered, versus 12.6% and 6.2% for explicit and compositional questions. Enterprise QA therefore depends not only on document recall, but also on whether implicit organizational relations become usable. The benchmark, data, and code are publicly available at ENTLORE.

</details>


### [229] [Deciding When to Rely on Visual Information: Gated Multimodal Fusion in Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10700)
*Natalija Glisovic,Danica Kragic,Martin Tegner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VisGate 是一种用于多模态序列推荐的框架，通过自适应的门控机制实现细粒度的视觉-协作信号融合，视觉效用被建模为依赖于项目和用户上下文的潜在变量，而非固定属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐系统通常统一融合视觉和协作信号，将视觉特征视为通用信息，忽略了视觉效用随项目和用户交互历史变化的上下文依赖性。

Method: 提出 VisGate 框架：基于项目嵌入和用户当前序列上下文做自适应的项目级融合决策；视觉表示通过序列共现模式上的对比目标学习，保持与协作嵌入的互补性而非对齐到共享空间；学习到的门控作为测量工具解释视觉信息何时何地有益。

Result: 在推荐性能上达到竞争水平；分析显示视觉效用因项目而异，在交互稀疏导致协作信号弱时增加，且与语义上有意义的视觉独特性相关。

Conclusion: 研究强调细粒度融合和模态互补性的重要性，证明项目级视觉效用可通过学习到的门控行为进行估计和解释。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommender systems commonly fuse visual and collaborative signals uniformly, treating visual features as generically informative regardless of item or user context. We argue that visual utility, defined as the contribution of visual signals to recommendation quality, is a latent contextual variable that depends on both the item and the user's interaction history rather than a fixed item property. To model this variability, we introduce VisGate, a framework that makes adaptive item-level fusion decisions conditioned on item embeddings and the user's current sequence context. Visual representations are learned through a contrastive objective over sequential co-occurrence patterns, preserving complementarity with collaborative embeddings rather than aligning them into a shared space. Beyond achieving competitive recommendation performance, VisGate's learned gate serves as a measurement tool for understanding when and why visual information is beneficial. Our analyses show that visual utility varies across items, increases under interaction sparsity when collaborative signals are weak, and correlates with visual distinctiveness in semantically meaningful ways. Together, these findings highlight the importance of both fine-grained fusion and modality complementarity, while demonstrating that item-level visual utility can be estimated and interpreted through learned gating behaviour.

</details>


### [230] [TimeRoute: Time-Aware Modality Routing and Diffusion for Multi-Modal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10983)
*Pengyu Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Klim Zaporojets,Congfeng Cao,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TimeRoute提出了一种基于扩散模型的多模态推荐系统，通过时感知模态路由器和FiLM条件化的双流去噪头，解决了模态效用随时间漂移的不匹配问题，在多个数据集上显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统融合协同信号与物品模态（文本、图像、音频），但各模态的效用随时间以不同速率漂移，导致两大耦合挑战：用户在不同时间语境下需要不同的模态比例，且低相关模态更易引入过时或误导性信号。现有方法使用全局共享的融合权重，无法应对这种时变特性。

Method: TimeRoute包含两大核心组件：1) 时感知模态路由器，将用户聚合行为特征映射为个性化模态分布，替代全局共享融合权重；2) 基于扩散的图重构器，通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) 以相同时间画像为条件，采用双流长短期去噪头，在传播图构建前抑制过时模态边。

Result: 在TikTok、Amazon-Baby、Amazon-Sports三个数据集上，通过10种随机种子的配对测试，TimeRoute在Recall@K、Precision@K和NDCG@K指标上均取得一致性提升，最高达9.8%，显著优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: TimeRoute通过统一的扩散框架有效解决了多模态推荐中的时间尺度不匹配问题，个性化的时感知模态路由与条件化去噪机制协同工作，能够动态适应模态效用的时变特性，为时感知多模态推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-modal recommenders fuse collaborative signals with item modalities such as text, images, and audio, but the usefulness of each drifts over time and at different rates. For example, chocolate purchases typically guided by textual ingredient cues can shift toward visual packaging and ambient audio around Valentine's Day. This modality time-scale mismatch gives rise to two coupled challenges: (1) users require different modality proportions across temporal contexts, and (2) less relevant modalities are more likely to introduce outdated or misleading signals into the recommender. We address both challenges within a unified diffusion-based recommender, TimeRoute. A temporal-aware modal router maps each user's aggregated behavioral features to a personalized modality distribution, replacing the globally shared fusion weights used in prior work. The diffusion-based graph reconstructor is then conditioned on the same temporal profile through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) with dual-stream long- and short-term denoising heads, suppressing outdated modality edges before they enter the propagation graph. Experiments on TikTok, Amazon-Baby, and Amazon-Sports demonstrate consistent improvements of up to 9.8\% in Recall@K, Precision@K, and NDCG@K over strong baselines across 10-seed paired tests. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TimeRoute.

</details>


### [231] [Sona Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11015)
*Sona Team,Alexandr Udeneev,Aleksei Krasilnikov,Alexey Nadtochiy,Andrey Semenov,Andrey Tsyrkunov,Anna Krivonos,Anna Lipkina,Artem Matveev,Daniil Burlakov,Daniil Leschev,Daria Tikhonovich,Denis Burshtein,Ekaterina Dmitrieva,Eugene Krofto,Grigorii Khlystov,Ilya Murzin,Kirill Golovko,Ksenia Sycheva,Leonid Dmitriev,Mariia Rozaeva,Mariia Ulianova,Mikhail Sandul,Nikolai Savushkin,Oleg Sorokin,Roman Odobesku,Semyon Panenko,Sergei Liamaev,Sergei Makeev,Vadim Shilov,Veronika Ivanova,Viktor Yanush,Vladimir Baikalov,Vladislav Dodonov,Vladislav Tytskiy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Yandex Music 推出 Sona，一个单模型生成式推荐系统，在线上 A/B 测试中替换了包含 15 多个候选生成器、预排序和排序模型的成熟级联系统，显著提升核心指标：活跃用户 +4.53%、总收听时长 +6.30%、点赞数 +11.42%，证明单一联合训练模型可超越复杂多级级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产级联系统极其复杂（15+ 候选生成器 + 预排序 + 排序，数百特征含大模型信号），维护成本高、延迟大；旨在用单一端到端生成式模型统一候选生成与排序，简化架构并提升推荐质量。

Method: Sona 架构：共享用户编码器将时序交互事件编码为隐状态，同时供自回归解码器（生成）和排序模块（打分）使用；联合优化 next-token-prediction（生成）与蒸馏目标（从大型 Teacher Ranker 学习排序），编码器参数共享耦合两任务；无手工特征，仅用事件字段与学习到的物品表示；推理时仅部署编码器、解码器和排序模块，教师模型仅训练期使用。

Result: 在“My Vibe”智能音箱场景的在线 A/B 实验中：活跃用户（主指标）+4.53%、总收听时长 +6.30%、点赞 +11.42%，均显著优于生产基线；活跃用户提升是此前最强模型 Argus 带来增量的 2.35 倍。

Conclusion: 单一联合训练的生成式模型可完全替代成熟的多级推荐级联，同时在线上流量显著提升推荐质量，验证了生成式推荐在工业级场景的可行性与优势。

Abstract: We introduce Sona, a single-model generative recommender for Yandex Music. In an online A/B test, Sona replaced the entire production cascade, comprising more than 15 candidate generators followed by pre-ranking and ranking models that consume hundreds of features, including signals from large transformer models such as Argus and target-attention scorers, while significantly improving key engagement metrics.
  The architecture of Sona unifies candidate generation and ranking around a shared user representation. Its encoder transforms the user's chronological sequence of logged engagement events into hidden states consumed by both the autoregressive decoder and the Ranking Module. The next-token-prediction and distillation objectives jointly update the encoder, coupling generation and ranking through the same user state. Neither Sona nor its Teacher Ranker uses hand-engineered features; both operate on logged event fields and learned item representations. In the final Sona configuration, the larger teacher supplies ranking targets during training but is absent from serving, leaving the encoder, decoder, and Ranking Module as a single deployed model.
  We evaluate Sona in an online A/B experiment using live traffic from My Vibe on smart speakers, one of Yandex Music's largest recommendation surfaces. Relative to the production control, Sona produced statistically significant uplifts of 4.53% in Active Users, the primary metric, 6.30% in Total Listening Time, and 11.42% in Likes. These effects were incremental to improvements retained from preceding deployments. The Active Users uplift was 2.35 times the increment previously delivered by Argus, the strongest model deployed on this surface before Sona. These results show that a single jointly trained model can replace a mature multi-stage recommendation cascade while improving recommendation quality on live traffic.

</details>


### [232] [Self-Knowledge Retrieval Augmented Generation Framework for Patent Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11030)
*Jian Zhang,Songlin Lei,Zhuohao Yang,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自我知识的RAG框架，用于专利检索与匹配，通过引导LLM自主抽取关键技术实体并构建层级本体结构，结合FAISS检索与生成式匹配机制，显著提升检索匹配准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的专利匹配方法依赖领域特定预训练或指令微调，存在人工标注成本高、灾难性遗忘等问题；传统RAG虽引入外部知识，但未充分利用LLM自主解析专利和挖掘深层语义关系的能力。

Method: 提出自我知识RAG框架：1) 引导LLM从专利匹配查询中自主抽取关键技术实体；2) 构建层级本体结构实现查询扩展；3) 集成FAISS向量检索与生成式匹配机制；4) 利用自我知识增强模型对专利创新点的理解。

Result: 在真实专利数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法表现优异，验证了其有效性和应用潜力。

Conclusion: 自我知识RAG框架通过LLM自主构建知识结构，有效解决了专利检索中的语义理解难题，为知识产权保护提供了更准确的技术支撑。

Abstract: Patent retrieval and matching based on large language models (LLMs) play a vital role in intellectual property protection. However, due to the complex structure of patent documents, dense technical terminology, and multi-modal information, traditional methods struggle to accurately identify subtle differences between patents. Existing LLM-based patent matching approaches typically rely on domain-specific pretrained or instruction tuning, which often entail high manual labeling costs and catastrophic forgetting. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods introduce external knowledge they fail to fully leverage LLM's capability to automatically parse patents and mine deep semantic relationships. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a self-knowledge RAG framework that guides LLMs to autonomously extract key technical entities and construct hierarchical ontological structures from patent matching queries, thereby enabling query expansion and precise retrieval. The method integrates the FAISS retrieval with a generative matching mechanism, leveraging self-knowledge to enhance the model's understanding of patent innovations and significantly improve retrieval and matching accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate the outstanding performance of the proposed method on real-world patent datasets, validating its effectiveness and application potential.

</details>


### [233] [Role of Personality in Conversational Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11164)
*Abdisalam Abukar,Junchen Fu,Chengli Zhai,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了助手人格（外向、尽责、中性）在会话式信息搜索中的作用，通过受试者内部实验发现：助手人格与任务类型交互显著影响用户信任与委托偏好，无单一最优人格，用户倾向于可选择或自适应的人格风格，表明助手人格应作为情境敏感的交互设计变量而非全局优化属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在信息搜索中扮演重要角色，不仅检索生成内容，还塑造用户表达约束、追问、验证及决策过程。然而，用户人格、助手人格与任务情境如何共同影响交互尚不明确。本文旨在将人格作为可控变量，研究其对用户行为和交互质量的影响。

Method: 采用受试者内部设计，26名参与者完成三类信息搜索任务（探索性旅行规划、比较性手机选购、验证敏感的健康饮食搜索），在三种助手人格条件（外向、尽责、中性）下交互。测量参与者大五人格分数，收集对话日志、行为轨迹、交互后问卷、退出问卷等数据。

Result: 三种助手人格在行为上显著不同：外向助手产生更长轮次，尽责助手引发更高用户词语占比和更多轮次，中性介于两者之间。最强效应为任务-助手交互对信任和委托的影响，偏好风格随任务变化。无全局最优人格，但参与者强烈偏好可选择或自适应的人格风格。

Conclusion: 助手人格应被视为情境敏感的交互设计变量，而非可全局优化的系统属性。未来系统应支持人格风格的用户选择或自适应调整，以匹配不同任务需求和用户偏好。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for information seeking, where users find, compare, and evaluate information through dialogue. In this role, the assistant does more than retrieve or generate content: it shapes how users articulate constraints, ask follow-up questions, verify claims, and decide when an answer is sufficient for action. Yet little is known about how user personality, assistant personality, and task context jointly influence these interactions. We examine personality as a controllable variable in conversational information seeking and study its effects on user behaviour and interaction quality. We conducted a controlled within-subject study in which assistant personality and task type were experimentally varied, while participant personality was measured using Big Five scores. Twenty-six participants each completed three information-seeking tasks under three assistant personality conditions: extraverted, conscientious, and neutral. Tasks covered exploratory travel planning, comparative smartphone shopping, and verification-sensitive health and diet information seeking. Data included conversation logs, behavioural traces, post-interaction questionnaires, an exit questionnaire, and Big Five measures. The assistant conditions were behaviourally distinct: the extraverted assistant produced longer turns, the conscientious assistant elicited higher user word share and more turns, and the neutral baseline fell between them. The strongest effect was a task-by-assistant interaction on trust and delegation, with preferred styles varying by task. No global winner emerged, but participants strongly preferred style choice or adaptation. These findings position assistant personality as a context-sensitive interactional design variable rather than a globally optimisable system property.

</details>


### [234] [Are We Really Making Progress in Group Recommendation? Unmasking the Tie-Breaking Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11190)
*Song-Duo Ma,Pu-Jen Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了群体推荐评估中存在的系统性偏差：训练时的额外 sigmoid 变换导致得分压缩，配合评估时的确定性打破平局，虚增了 HR@K 和 NDCG@K 等指标。作者提出了感知平局的评估协议（均匀随机打破平局下的期望指标），重新评估了多个近期方法，发现许多先前报告的改进大幅缩水，方法排名也发生显著变化，并证明温度缩放的 BPR 可在不引入严重平局膨胀的情况下保留隐式边界平滑的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期群体推荐方法在标准基准上报告了显著提升，但不清楚这些增益是否真正反映了对群体偏好的建模进步。论文旨在揭示并量化由训练时得分压缩与评估时确定性打破平局交互导致的系统性评估偏差，建立可靠的进展衡量标准。

Method: 1. 分析额外 sigmoid 变换在 BPR 目标前如何导致顶部得分大量打平。2. 提出感知平局评估协议：在均匀随机打破平局下计算 HR@K 和 NDCG@K 的确切期望。3. 在 CAMRa2011 和 Mafengwo 数据集上，对多个代表性近期方法及其基线进行群体推荐与用户推荐双设置的重新评估。4. 探究 sigmoid 的隐式边界平滑作用，并提出温度缩放 BPR 作为替代方案。

Result: 在感知平局评估下，许多先前报告的改进大幅收缩，方法间的相对排名发生显著变化。温度缩放 BPR 能在不引入严重平局膨胀的情况下，保留 sigmoid 带来的大部分隐式边界平滑优势。

Conclusion: 感知平局的评估对于建立群体推荐领域可靠的进展至关重要。未来工作应采用该协议以避免虚假的性能提升，并考虑温度缩放 BPR 作为更稳健的训练目标。

Abstract: Recent group recommendation methods have reported strong improvements on standard benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains always reflect genuine advances in modeling group preferences. In this paper, we show that several recent methods are affected by a systematic evaluation bias caused by the interaction between training-time score compression and evaluation-time deterministic tie-breaking. Specifically, an additional sigmoid transformation before the BPR objective can greatly increase tied top scores, making top-K metrics such as HR@K and NDCG@K highly sensitive to how ties are resolved. We revisit recent representative methods and their baselines on CAMRa2011 and Mafengwo under both group and user recommendation settings, and evaluate them with a tie-aware protocol that computes the exact expectation of HR@K and NDCG@K under uniform random tie-breaking. Our results show that many previously reported improvements shrink substantially under tie-aware evaluation, and the relative ranking of methods can change markedly. We further show that the additional sigmoid may act as implicit margin smoothing during optimization, and that temperature-scaled BPR can retain much of this benefit without inducing severe tie inflation. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of tie-aware evaluation for establishing reliable progress in group recommendation. The code is available at https://github.com/songduoma/TieAwareGroupRec.

</details>


### [235] [Do LLM Recommenders Know When They're Hallucinating? Auditing Confidence Calibration in Catalog Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10008)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文联合审计了四大零样本LLM推荐系统的幻觉率(OOD@10)与口头置信度校准(ECE、Brier、可靠性)，发现幻觉率强依赖目录(0~8.4%)，但即使幻觉为零，口头置信度也显著欠校准(ECE高达0.223)。四模型在所有12个实验单元中系统性地表现出“欠自信”：口头均值67~86，实际准确率却达92~100%。这与常见的“过自信”刻板印象相反，本质是“诱导错位” —— 通用置信提示引出的是推荐质量评分，而非目录成员概率。共形弃权阈值仅能以4~21 pp的覆盖率代价降低≤0.7 pp幻觉，因欠自信通道无法分离正确项与幻觉。建议审计同时报告校准指标与OOD，并采用目录锚定的诱导方式替代通用置信提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐审计仅以二元域外率(OOD)衡量幻觉，忽略模型是否“知晓”自身幻觉。论文旨在联合审计幻觉率与口头置信度校准，揭示模型主观确信度与客观正确性的关系，并评估基于置信度的弃权策略是否有效。

Method: 选取四家厂商的零样本LLM推荐器(Mistral Large、Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Sonnet 4.6)，在三个真实目录(MovieLens-25M、Amazon Reviews 2023 Toys、Yelp Open Dataset)上进行Top-K推荐，按物品流行度分层。指标包括OOD@10(幻觉率)、ECE/Brier/可靠性图(校准)、共形弃权阈值下的幻觉降低与覆盖率损失。

Result: 1. 幻觉率强依赖目录：MovieLens 0~0.2%、Amazon 4.5~8.3%、Yelp 2.2~8.4%。2. 即使OOD=0，口头置信度仍严重欠校准(ECE高达0.223)。3. 四模型在所有12单元系统性欠自信：口头均值67~86，实际准确率92~100%。4. 共形弃权(α=0.05~0.20)仅减少≤0.7 pp幻觉，却损失4~21 pp覆盖率，因欠自信通道无法区分正确项与幻觉。

Conclusion: LLM推荐审计必须同时报告校准指标与OOD；通用“Just Ask”置信提示诱导出的是推荐质量评分而非目录成员概率，导致系统性欠自信。应改用目录锚定的诱导方式，并谨慎使用基于口头置信度的弃权机制。

Abstract: LLM recommenders for top-$K$ item suggestion regularly emit titles outside the target catalog. Prior audits measure this as a binary out-of-domain rate; none ask whether the model knew it was hallucinating. We jointly audit hallucination rate (OOD@10) and verbalized-confidence calibration (ECE, Brier, reliability) for four zero-shot LLM recommenders from four independent vendors (Mistral Large, Llama-3.3-70B, GPT-OSS-120B, Claude Sonnet 4.6), not grounded or fine-tuned systems, across three catalogs (MovieLens-25M, Amazon Reviews 2023 Toys, Yelp Open Dataset), stratified by item popularity. Hallucination is catalog-dependent (0--0.2\% on MovieLens, 4.5--8.3\% on Amazon, 2.2--8.4\% on Yelp), but verbalized confidence is materially miscalibrated even when hallucination is zero (ECE up to 0.223 on MovieLens despite 0\% OOD). All four LLMs are systematically \emph{under}-confident across all twelve cells, verbalizing a mean of 67--86 on items they recommend with 92--100\% accuracy. This is the opposite of the over-confidence usually emphasized in LLM-hallucination work. The under-confidence is best read as an \emph{elicitation mismatch}: ``Just Ask'' elicits a generic recommendation-quality rating, not a catalog-membership probability. A conformal abstention threshold over verbalized confidence reduces hallucination by at most 0.7\,pp across $α\in \{.05, .10, .15, .20\}$, at 4--21\,pp of coverage cost: the under-confident channel cannot separate correct items from hallucinations, so the threshold mostly removes correct items. We recommend that audits of LLM recommenders report calibration alongside OOD, and use catalog-anchored elicitation rather than generic confidence prompts.

</details>


### [236] [Sequential Modality Dropout for Robust Multi-Modal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10240)
*Guanqun Yang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了顺序模态丢弃 (SMD)，一种简单且与架构无关的训练技术，通过在训练期间随机擦除整个用户交互历史中的模态流（图像/文本），使多模态顺序推荐模型对部署时的缺失模态具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实产品目录常缺失图像或文本模态，而在完整数据上训练的多模态顺序推荐模型在服务时遇到模态缺失会导致推荐准确率大幅下降。现有方法未充分解决此鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出顺序模态丢弃 (SMD)：训练时以概率 p 独立擦除每个模态流（图像和文本）的整个用户交互历史，强制模型学习不依赖任何单一模态进行预测。可选跨模态重构损失进一步提升鲁棒性。仅需四行代码修改，与架构无关。

Result: 在四个主流骨干网络 (MM-SASRec, IISAN, MISSRec, fMRLRec) 和四个 Amazon 领域实验中，SMD 将文本保留率提升 1.0 到 3.2 倍，且不损害全模态准确率；极端 95% 逐项缺失率下保留 61% HR@10（对比无 SMD 的 22%，提升 2.8 倍）。跨模态重构损失在简单加性骨干上将严重文本缺失下的保留率从 90% 提升至 98%。

Conclusion: SMD 是一种极简、架构无关的训练策略，有效解决了多模态顺序推荐在部署时面临的模态缺失问题，显著提升了模型在缺失模态下的推荐鲁棒性，几乎不牺牲全模态性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal sequential recommenders assume every item carries every modality, but real product catalogs often miss images or text, and a model trained on complete data loses much of its recommendation accuracy when a modality is unavailable at serving time. We propose Sequential Modality Dropout (SMD): during training, each modality stream (image and text) is independently erased with probability p for an entire user interaction history, so the model learns to predict the next item without relying on any single modality. We measure robustness by retention, the fraction of a model's full-modality accuracy (HR@10) that survives when a modality is removed at test time. Across four backbones (MM-SASRec, IISAN, MISSRec, and fMRLRec) on four Amazon domains, SMD raises text retention by 1.0 to 3.2x at essentially no cost to full-modality accuracy; under an extreme 95% per-item missing rate, it retains 61% of HR@10 versus 22% without (a 2.8x improvement). An optional cross-modal reconstruction loss further lifts retention from 90% to 98% on a simple additive backbone under severe text missingness. SMD is a four-line, architecture-agnostic change that makes multi-modal sequential recommenders robust to the missing modalities they actually encounter in deployment.

</details>


### [237] [DualSpectralCF: Training-Free Sign-Aware Spectral Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10247)
*Guanqun Yang,Tong Qi,Xiaoxue Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DualSpectralCF是一个无需训练的符号感知协同过滤框架，通过引入带符号的输入信号和物品-物品算子，将显式负反馈融入谱方法中，在保持极高推理速度的同时显著提升推荐精度，尤其对冷启动用户效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统面临两难：基于梯度的有符号推荐模型虽能利用显式负反馈（如差评、低观看比）提升精度，但训练成本高；无训练谱协同过滤方法虽高效且精度媲美甚至超越有监督图模型，却仅利用正向交互。论文旨在弥合这两条技术路线，实现无训练、高效且能利用负反馈的推荐。

Method: 提出DualSpectralCF框架，包含两个即插即用组件：带符号输入信号$r_u^±$编码用户显式厌恶，带符号物品-物品算子$M^±$融合“同好相似”与“同恶相似”。框架骨干无关，仅引入两个标量超参数$(γ, κ)$，可接入任意形式为$ř_u = F(M) r_u$的谱骨干（如ChebyCF、GF-CF、Turbo-CF）。

Result: 在5个带符号基准上，所有实例均匹配或超越无符号骨干，Recall@20最高提升+32.6%（骨干特定调参）与+1.9%~+16.0%（固定默认参数）；比SIGformer快7.7~155.3倍，精度达其70.7%~90.7%；对冷启动用户（Epinions 1~5条训练项）Recall@20最高提升+29.2%。

Conclusion: DualSpectralCF以极低开销实现了符号感知谱协同过滤，兼顾了训练自由、推理极快、精度显著提升（尤其冷启动场景）三大优势，为工业界大规模负反馈利用提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Real-world recommendation platforms routinely collect explicit negative feedback such as 1-star reviews, hate-button clicks, distrust between users, and very-low watch-ratio videos. Learned sign-aware recommenders exploit this signal for clear accuracy gains, but only at the cost of gradient-based training. In parallel, a line of training-free spectral collaborative filtering methods matches or beats learned graph recommenders at a fraction of the cost, yet operates on positive interactions alone. We bridge these two lines with DualSpectralCF, a training-free framework of two components that attach to any spectral backbone of the form $\hat{\mathbf{r}}_u = F(\mathbf{M}) \mathbf{r}_u$: a signed input signal $\mathbf{r}_u^{\pm}$ that encodes the user's explicit dislikes, and a signed item-item operator $\mathbf{M}^{\pm}$ that blends like-together and dislike-together similarity. The framework is backbone-agnostic and adds just two scalar hyperparameters. We instantiate DualSpectralCF on ChebyCF, GF-CF, and Turbo-CF, and evaluate on five sign-aware benchmarks: every instance matches or beats its unsigned backbone on all 5 datasets, with Recall@20 lifts up to +32.6% with backbone-specific $(γ, κ)$ tuning and +1.9% to +16.0% for DualSpectralCF-Cheby at the fixed default $(γ= -0.5, κ= 0.1)$, and the family runs 7.7 to 155.3$\times$ faster than SIGformer while reaching 70.7% to 90.7% of its accuracy. Sign-awareness helps most for cold-start users, with up to +29.2% Recall@20 on Epinions users with 1 to 5 training items.

</details>
